                                💡 AI Executive Summary: Key News Insights
                            </div>
                            <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" width="100%" style="background-color: #F8FAFC; border-left: 4px solid #FF9900; border: 1px solid #E2E8F0; border-left-width: 4px;">
                                <tr>
                                    <td style="padding: 20px 24px; font-size: 13.5px; color: #334155; line-height: 1.7em; text-align: justify;">
                                        <div style='margin-top:2px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>1. 러시아의 우크라이나 데이터센터 군사 타격과 물리적 인프라 안보화</div>현대 하이브리드전에서 데이터센터가 군사적 표적으로 급부상하며 물리적 인프라 방호가 국가 안보의 선결 과제로 직결됨. 러시아군이 우크라이나 기간 통신망을 겨냥해 미사일·드론 공습을 단행하며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMixwFBVV95cUxOMzNYXzZmVUxfWFM4bHFTcUpnN0VKSDFxX3RtdmN6RzlSMXJOX04yQWxjOXByaGNTMmdPYnhpRGhKbEhIWGp5X0UzeFZKWnZ0S0dLVVdWTkJiX0doUkNEbjQxelIweHBIOEJmRU50TVRWLTludHN5RHh3U3Vhd1JucjNCWkZWZ0dkMEo3ZEpuLTVvdG9RdGl5MnE3T0lEaEVha1JfeGoyYU1sXzVYeml6QU11RnZ6Qnpla3NJYlhGTEpndTdnMFpB?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[1]</a> 키이우의 코스모노바(Cosmonova) 데이터센터를 폭격해 가동을 전면 중단시킴 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiqAFBVV95cUxPNmtfa0JkZU9qMW9VY1dEcVVkSHNBZDdMZWpVZGt1ek5EenFyRkRuSmRZNnp6clBGY3VsMmRLY3BNNTVuVUpSbi1lRjdzMy1MTGdIaDVSdzd3ZmdvZERFNjVvMDAtS1N4QVM3QUdEQ19id1p5TXh0cVVoOW1hbU5udjdpUDItb1ZaS25HMUVDbFNyZmZRcDd3STM3ZHZTZFlMRUx5QV94bmjSAagBQVVfeXFMTzZrX2tCZGVPajFvVWNXRHFVZEhzQWQ3TGVqVWRrdXpORHpxckZEbkpkWTZ6enJQRmN1bDJkS2NwTTU1blVKUm4tZUY3czMtTExnSGg1Unc3d2Znb2RERTY1bzAwLUtTeEFTN0FHRENfYndaeU14dHFVaDltYW1ObnY3aVAyLW9WWktuRzFFQ2xTcmZmUXA3d0kzN2R2U2RZTEVMeUFfeG5o?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[2]</a>. 동시에 국영 통신사 데이터그룹(Datagroup) 센터가 파괴되고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMilAFBVV95cUxPbVQzOU5yQ1QySDhHaHBxeTYtRmJUZ3Y1ZG82ajBlNnU1aTZWNDFLdjNST28zUTJrNzhERzBWWTVRMWRjemtpaU1wZ3k0U3cxelZZUHAxRGJZc2E3SW5QT2RQbWpIWmRsaElRcDk4OTJIQzEyRWxZZ0ZfQVN5RDIwaWJEX3A3QlBobXU3bURRakRfYjli?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[3]</a> 스타링크 거점까지 피격되며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMikAFBVV95cUxQTmRHMjFkLU1rNFhib0ZXa0JSQ09FeDQ0V0l3M1RuLXljUDdVUGNILUthODMySkc4Sl93YmNYRmYtS29IbmxBSHBUWDBMdEJzRHZBQ181emJpcHgyXzRvTDJISGJzOTV3V1F0Tk5BYkNfUkNKR3pXaFpmVTZUaHJrTG5PeFh2OTJYU3oyUEp5dnk?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[4]</a> 10만 가구의 인터넷과 방송이 마비되는 혼란이 발생함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMi6gFBVV95cUxQNUxvTjY5Y29rZGZSYVNyNHRCeDdtUHVScHZGUG9wUEcteGVIdEN2RnB3SEJMdThSU2d6M0ZZMlV5d0RaQ2FXWHBTaVJXcGNCTDVibzUwRldLQTZ1NXBsVE5vbGFWblhnWGstTjVqcTBvTXhfdmlBeFQxaGU1X0Qydk5pNmNTV3hQQWpNY095UlhnTjhoYUFnb2ZnMXRVUzNjWHhpXzYyMjFMWnJjd0Zma1p1QndEQzF0aGE5YWx4YnRoNEdYSnltdnlzeEFrekF1ZUZtekoxakJjZThHcDNIaV9rN245R01Xenc?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[5]</a>. 이에 젤렌스키 우크라이나 대통령은 인프라 군사 방호 강화를 선언했으며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMilgFBVV95cUxQTkhXTVlNdXJ1c2xMUnlIdnNaY1dRRF85VHdyam9IOGJtaEY4RzFuUzFvZzVjRUUySE9sOFYwZnd2dWUxeGpOV0NscTJ1eklGT1QwRm5WYjlqOVV4MFp0cFBEUmJxZDlUallDUXVNdWhhdEo0OEN3MmR1MUFwNFpLYVRBa19PbWlsTENPQXRES3ctS3JDTHc?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[6]</a> 업계는 대드론 다층 방어 체계 구축에 착수함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMikwJBVV95cUxPZXFrem02SDBSdHEza2dmT3hVSXlHNkdqb3preEIwWU4tVGRfcEl4eXp4RE9Xb0lBZG92aTZDVGtHV3RhRjdHcjA5Qml4WHFEN0xmVHhUUHJ1VnVwemJQY2lPeW12SURja0RCNmo3WHFSRVEtd0lfcmNQU1o0bmtnQ3IyZ0NVN0JKNHBSeGN6amZ1LXVXRXg2X1VUSDZGWEJoWXJuT0Zoc3ZFVkFLaFk2S3V1MktBX0pmVk5QdmQ1eWNQZWNLem1kTEtaUmUtQ0JQN0s0WnZFSExBb1hYenZ4TzNQQWQ4VlZIWUZxNnBNbTFLYzlIWGpiNGhzWGZzWlF5TXdJZVplbnI4ckZXcFJpb2hza9IB4gFBVV95cUxQTkRSZ2dBaHBoWGtWV19Ed3ZBc1h5UUpINmZCUEU3ZWF6R0ZhMVNrdGgzY1hXcWRyNWJsMTBYTHprTUdiUUdTZHM0SmJlVEVfMEZqcERINjJremZsb3VkOE5jVEU4MzJLNFNvWUkwVXFIdjF2WUdjcGZiWVRGSVlwbjhGTExHeWt1T3Zoc2RRU0NjTU1sT3FKb3dCOXl3N1VMUDF0d19sWmZZZC1uSVpLTEc3VG5UVVBuYWRPTzlFWjRsM0Z0MTFKdFQxSkNQblNXQW1KWm1pU0VPNU5lWVJOQlZ3?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[7]</a>. 국내에서도 국정자원 화재 교훈을 바탕으로 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=(국정자원 화재 1년) `3시간 복구`라더니 10.8일…709개 중 DR로 살린 건 7개&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[8]</a> 방위사업청이 18년 만에 망분리를 완화하며 국방 클라우드 전환을 추진하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiW0FVX3lxTFBCYmFFLXM1bUZxRElGX0FJa1FNTDNlZ3FtQkdBUGZ0bFF3TTJSTzJyRGxySURCUEkxX1JxMEZKOTE0dG9IeUJORV8xSHUwUE1Sck53Nk1hT2pNdk0?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[9]</a> 국정원이 보안체계를 개편하는 등 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiZEFVX3lxTE1KN2tuTHpMckFqS2c2bnFfUEROa1hwNXdnWDMyWXNTN1VOcWY2RWkyLU9BQm54QWVMODUyX29va3BMTjN0U2xIbFlrOTN2MnZKYUpvUDB1R19ZdDBUdUx2Slh4Nmk?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[10]</a> 유사시 국가 데이터 주권과 복원력 사수에 속도를 냄.<div style='margin-top:18px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>2. 아마존-제너랙 80억 달러 비상발전 계약과 미 에너지부 송전망 투자</div>공공 전력망의 송배전 병목으로 데이터센터 가동이 위협받자 대용량 비상 발전 설비 선점과 계통 보강이 핵심 해법으로 부상함. 아마존 AWS는 전력난에 대응해 발전기 제조사 제너랙(Generac)과 2027~2028년 24억 달러(약 3조 2,400억 원) 공급을 시작으로 최대 80억 달러(약 10조 8,000억 원) 규모의 백업 발전기 구매 계약을 체결함 <a href='https://www.datacenterfrontier.com/energy/article/55407678/amazon-generac-deal-puts-backup-power-in-the-ai-infrastructure-spotlight' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[11]</a>. 아마존은 주당 약 200.93달러에 제너랙 주식 169만 주를 인수할 워런트를 확보해 <a href='https://www.datacenterfrontier.com/energy/article/55407678/amazon-generac-deal-puts-backup-power-in-the-ai-infrastructure-spotlight' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[11]</a> 장비 공급망과 금융 가치를 결합함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMif0FVX3lxTFBiSm9vemJNY1FZdUZ3Zk9iX1Q5X3NTQWtJUkhmZWxIWXFnRE5KVnd1cl9tNndhUEFuM0xWZ3JGM1NZVnI2aDBLSVBjaDI1aGJrTGdTMDRQbjJGVTZ5RnVrV0ZIc2oxVzc5azJLaVNRVXVNVVlyWU5Oc3U5dVZDN1E?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[12]</a>. 미 에너지부(DOE) 역시 계통 과부하를 풀기 위해 31개 사업에 19억 달러(약 2조 5,600억 원)를 지원하고 <a href='https://www.datacenterdynamics.com/en/news/doe-unveils-19bn-in-funding-for-31-grid-upgrade-projects-to-speed-data-center-connections/' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[13]</a> 52억 5,000만 달러(약 7조 800억 원) 규모의 송전망 확충에 돌입함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMi6wFBVV95cUxQald4MzRZWnpUMHVVdnpVdy1ZNnI2Rlc5QllLdkxybndoekVWMTZ6TVVvcUZGUzhlV016STB0cDNma2QyU3FJREllMHphRUcyNmpWM05KVDM0aFZJSThzV2ZPRjg4bnlwb1VtWDVMTzRWXzE2Mjh3VGQwaVFhZXUybnAtTjlqZmpNWEF3T1o5UEktUzhiQlFYQzNVSGxiLVZPTEhhUnAxT3lWSFB0TU9sOHJaQU9KSUowdzFrRXNBR3V6SVhGYVJkMXZxUHZQeU85d3cwWjhqRGRIVWNlUkZ4ZGlFQXZsdC15N280?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[14]</a>. 반면 크루소(Crusoe)가 12억 5,000만 달러(약 1조 6,900억 원) 규모의 붐 가스터빈 계약을 철회하며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiowFBVV95cUxNTG1DRm12U1JjYks1dXo2emhyZjJ1Znk2dXBWNXBSQXVrSjI1VTRiTjFNVkxDUVZhaG9QeFdMUGo4eW13aWZ2bHh6c18yakFPVzJQa1JaODBoRjROeDMzdzFuWlFja0ZBanVSZHVFVnZLRTgwUXJuUW5WdVpWU3hVNWFNWTF3R3hqc1FZd1Z5NWg0enFzVDNGa0NVMTMzMkx5anhv?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[15]</a> 자체 발전의 변동성도 노출됨. 국내에서는 K-배터리 3사가 북미 데이터센터향 BESS 투자를 확대하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMicEFVX3lxTE5Ma1lfeEcxLXNBSlh4SlVyci11SmQzZ1VFcWRjeUlNMW10LTByZFZyREs1RTdmLWVqeVBxVTllWUFwSFIxY2NwQVd5NlZoSlhiQVRiYTlqUGFVV09QRmQ5cS1GaGpoMUktcXp1ZDZkQWnSAXRBVV95cUxQY0lBSkxmM2VWemlMZVFST05WRERiOWxMbWI1YW5LNXlwMkF1ZFdxNnp5TjY1ZHNkWnlha3A1bVlWOGtHejE5X04zeloxdFFodlRWRTRKOEszSG52UFNXRXZZM0UxMHJhNktpMkpWeE1keTE2MA?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[16]</a> 전력기기 3사의 초고압 변압기 수주잔고가 50조 원을 돌파하며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiY0FVX3lxTFBUUFZYTTY0ZGc2aHI5bllyZ2FlUG5NMDNPOWhqa29VaDBpdmVQbnAzeHVNVFZZVXBRMzhHallqaGJ3TmtHSEFRNkE2aEptX1ZLTzlINDE1X0VZV1R1dFFVRzVjUdIBbEFVX3lxTE9aNm5sUXNNSnlOMlVNZmx6QkFUT0poNGE5bkg3OUtUUjNFNzFvQlg4bEZIVjVCZ0FxUm1pZmRCalI2NkpXVVVIUFN6dmJ3UzJkRXRvVTNUUTBGeVl2X0c1dGNXYUZBN3pZRXdsYw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[17]</a> 글로벌 계통망 확충의 핵심 공급망으로 도약함.<div style='margin-top:18px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>3. LG전자 엔비디아 NPN 냉각 파트너 등재와 메가와트급 액체냉각 격돌</div>AI 칩셋의 고집적화로 랙당 전력 밀도가 100kW를 넘어서며 칩 표면부터 칠러까지 열을 식히는 액체냉각 기술이 인프라 주도권의 분수령으로 부상함. LG전자는 엔비디아 파트너 네트워크(NPN)의 '전력 및 냉각' 부문에서 최상위 등급인 프리퍼드(Preferred) 파트너로 공식 등재됨 <a href='https://news.einfomax.co.kr/news/articleView.html?idxno=4436566' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[18]</a>. 이로써 LG전자는 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러의 데이터센터 기획·설계 초기 단계부터 참여할 적격성을 공인받음 <a href='https://news.einfomax.co.kr/news/articleView.html?idxno=4436566' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[18]</a>. LG전자는 600kW 및 1MW급 CDU 규격을 충족한 데 이어 <a href='https://news.einfomax.co.kr/news/articleView.html?idxno=4436566' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[18]</a> 2.5MW급 설비까지 엔비디아 DSX 레디 승인을 획득해 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMid0FVX3lxTFBUSnpZRDFyazRjYTRJcWU0bDh3UkhBVktFblY0MlhDUmJId0VTLVptM0IwcTNyQ2pFbTFYdk5BdHRRQXZIN1I1b3BfUEd2WjYyVXYzczZKT25Sa3VialY0QXlxX2ZaVmZlNHBUTGt1VlgxMEFhSk9B0gF3QVVfeXFMUFRKellEMXJrNGNhNElxZTRsOHdSSEFWS0VuVjQyWENSYkh3RVMtWm0zQjBxM3JDakVtMVh2TkF0dFFBdkg3UjVvcF9QR3ZaNjJVdjNzNkpPblJrdWJqVjRBeXFfZlpWZmU0cFRMa3VWWDEwQWFKT0E?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[19]</a> 칩 투 칠러(Chip to Chiller) 라인업을 완성함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMibEFVX3lxTFBMaDlITFNDX3VOdHZja3ZFVDNoNy02Nk1URWNHaU5BaGFob3pGcHo0LVY1ZHV2N2huWFlLTzFOd0NxUl9GM0VNY3dlNmFPOHRCWkZhYjJ4R0JGM0dhVk5FM2dOczdWQVo5UFc3cw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[20]</a>. 글로벌 시장에서는 버티브가 쿨칩(CoolChip) CDU 인증으로 맞불을 놓고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMikwFBVV95cUxQNml1QXFTY0QtZFd3ellYaDk5YkJLbTRldHdUaFZFMmNRbHV4ODV1bGEwTGZESjJiN1M4dzhqdm9pQ0FnY0FIa0tkVFdsSlZwQ3JSWmJfbnI0X09ZLU81RVZfYkZwMmtrb2RrSFB5cFBtTmlJMEVmTTlMUW1ZTU5ISzlqQklrOXZkMEN6Q0xtNEg5OXc?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[21]</a> 슈나이더 일렉트릭이 프리팹 모듈러 시스템을 확장하며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMitwFBVV95cUxQaDY4U0FXakp5VnlOSDlzWFMxRGJsV2ZJUmtWNHJ2UWYxNEhwOEx5TjBrS1B4NjQtTnY1UTd3RDFUdDd5bHJLRzJtU0V3anRrY2t1LW9JLUNwdG1UdGw1N3gxamFrT2VaS2xUb3ZJTWFnVjBLaGVRQUdLNTB0M09STzVmdHRNX0JnT3BaMU1velJFYnluQldrQVZpekNKd2VjcXBpVXpfNVY0ZW1ua3pxLW5nUWFmNlU?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[22]</a> 경쟁을 가열시킴. 국내에서도 삼성전자가 광주 라인을 가동해 공조 설비 공급망을 넓히며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMieEFVX3lxTE8zX09sM19OREp4NDhHamRIeTFWMWVhNDh6OElFWkRERkZXaDdPb0ZWOUlvdFZDWXR1R3ZsSUo5Y29xZ21maDBTcm50OHloNU5qN2t2aVBBQlZ6NFFxaHlwWmFVZi1ZSzZDdGt3WlpwSVRuSGJ1WUFBWtIBeEFVX3lxTE8zX09sM19OREp4NDhHamRIeTFWMWVhNDh6OElFWkRERkZXaDdPb0ZWOUlvdFZDWXR1R3ZsSUo5Y29xZ21maDBTcm50OHloNU5qN2t2aVBBQlZ6NFFxaHlwWmFVZi1ZSzZDdGt3WlpwSVRuSGJ1WUFBWg?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[23]</a> LG전자와 메가와트급 CDU 수주전에 돌입했고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiVEFVX3lxTE5ra0Z0em50cDVvbUU0cno5Z0lNY2U5Z3FCWjYxWU5uTmxYLVhzYksxRVM4VjlMSHFRYVZYOFFhWkwyRnZvMUFyWDhpWGJCanZFM0ZMSQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[24]</a> 매니코어소프트가 액체냉각 서버 처리량 세계 1위를 달성하는 등 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiZEFVX3lxTE83dHdLRzNFS1hTdy1nQURwMVFxSzNFbHFNWGZqU3hCdm1OQUl2QUFkalZkcnpvVk16VzVZOHNrRGtpZkdyMkE3UDNHVkhLZERnU2t3WVI2V1ZTeG5qTHhBU2pCQ1U?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[25]</a> 차세대 열관리가 하드웨어 수주를 결정짓는 핵심 척도로 안착함.<div style='margin-top:18px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>4. 구글-페르보 900MW 지열발전 가동과 계통 규제 속 연안형 분산 입지</div>송배전 계통의 극심한 정체와 환경 규제가 맞물리자 무탄소 24시간 기저전원인 지열발전 상용화와 분산형 대안 입지 확보가 가속화됨. 구글이 지원하는 페르보 에너지(Fervo Energy)는 미국 유타주 900MW급 케이프 스테이션에서 세계 최초로 유틸리티 규모 심부 지열 발전에 성공함 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Google-backed Fervo Energy achieves first power at up to 900MW Cape Station geothermal plant in Utah&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[26]</a>. 이는 구글이 체결한 396MW 규모 장기 전력구매계약(PPA)을 토대로 가동되며 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Google-backed Fervo Energy achieves first power at up to 900MW Cape Station geothermal plant in Utah&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[26]</a> 무탄소 기저전력 조달의 실증 모델을 구축함 <a href='https://www.theregister.com/systems/2026/09/25/google-backed-energy-outfit-brings-33-mw-of-4-gw-geothermal-potential-online-in-utah/5299243' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[27]</a>. 구글은 월 3,000조 개 이상의 AI 토큰 처리에 대응해 <a href='https://www.datacenterknowledge.com/energy-power-supply/google-s-grid-interactive-ai-data-centers-from-backup-to-grid-partner' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[28]</a> 저전압 직류(LVDC)와 BESS를 결합한 '그리드 인터랙티브' 모델을 도입하며 계통망과 상생을 꾀함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiwwFBVV95cUxNUlBQWjQ5dTNydHZjZGZPUGJBZkY4dE5CNTU4eW91VlA5TjdYWERhVGRfWVhsd3NCbDBOMV96UWRrMDVBOXVmOVJCOFFYTWpyQURRemotSlJvYlczbEpiWWp2MDRQdmxHRWFRUmF6eURzeXdTaGRnWllmajI4WWFsbXJvLXN4Sl9ldDFNdW5LbElXcDRpZ00xS2l0N2IzMFFKYUJyT1hMdVA3Nkl6RDIzMVp3WHNma0FHV1BOX2h3VFhrX2M?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[29]</a>. 반면 캘리포니아주가 7건의 규제법을 제정하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiigFBVV95cUxPVnZjekhfYVN5dkI3Sldldl9tTnprTldnZFNHMDRfQ00zU2R6T1IwYldQRkFVUE1SOFVFSGhIZEdtR1Y5X0JaOFlwSkJQeGg1LWFPMm1YWkNUdHJUWWZDS3dEZ3pROWlmelVTd1VMcmxXeUEtTHNqbGpVWmZzN3NsVXo3VG9GMjZKNGfSAY8BQVVfeXFMT3ZBeGxjUDI5V0FJa2FvWjBWdFF1clFyam5kYmdXSDNieUJnaUpQaVFHbklpM1h6X2dfN055aEhCbG56ZFJwc0NhODRHS3lRSk9MMXVkeWJxQzdJRnpvMnRVX0tFY2s1T2xjSFlFYXEzbU1fT2lEX3RiNFBkVlBTMnVqRnV3eDZnajU1cWpXOVU?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[30]</a> 호주 빅토리아주가 재생에너지 자급을 의무화하며 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Victoria, Australia, proposes rules requiring data centers to secure own renewable energy supply&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[31]</a> 텍사스주가 수백 개 시설의 용수를 조사하는 등 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMi8wFBVV95cUxPQlhEaXlHZ2QycjI3eGZXTy1LS19aTk40ZzcxWTVxdThubkd5UTZjYWV3d0g2V1BvcWJreUU4d2lWRGZiV0tuTFV6U1c5T2FMZHZGcDNMRFZXb3pyRW82SklWLTNDQndNd0swc21QbFp1OEl3ZExZUWl6V1JGWnNyeU14cDZ6YWp2cUhFMUlEV2lYNEhpbENFb3lrb3RXalZ3cXVtTUZneWxWRlFPdmIwbDVMcUtiUnBXRlltdlRxUENGSUdNTzNZOVVyaEdaazBWZVI0SFlhV0ppaXNpWkZnb3FOaFgtNzB4emZTZTNsYkxaZFE?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[32]</a> 규제 장벽도 심화됨. 국내에서는 수도권 계통 포화를 우회하고자 강원 강릉에 1GW급 AI 데이터센터가 착공에 들어가고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiY0FVX3lxTFBuclNxVEpoclZxTFJIeVMwdmVkQzgtTk4zRlN5MUY0X1R3RUItSHdzNVp3anlIeW1CTmRHa1BBMWN3Z3E2NDF3NVF5ZXBONm5RSGRnOEgwaWFWWjJzRFF4aU1RYw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[33]</a> 삼척블루파워 부지 유치가 타진되는 가운데 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiRkFVX3lxTE1POF9kTXZtNmIyQlhqWVk3NmlVS1htRUphRTVBelRqc0ZtMmFZZmgyVkwtV0ZlcWhEYXNQZ2tYTzc3WUpCT1E?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[34]</a> 해상 부유식 데이터센터 실증이 추진되는 등 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=바다로 간 챗GPT -> 삼성중공업·오픈AI ‘수상한 동맹’의 실체 => 뭍에서 쫓겨난 AI 데이터센터 >> 거제 앞바다서 부활의 닻 올린다 전력망 블랙아웃과 용수 대란 끝낼 승부수 = 해상 부유식 DC’가 온다 |&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[35]</a> 연안 분산형 인프라가 실질적 돌파구로 부상함.
